Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the acoustics structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 565. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 168 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 565 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the acoustics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 168. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
